# Agents · R · part 38

954 entries, revit-developer to risk-profiler-agent. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **revit-developer**: Implements C# / .NET 8 code against the Revit 2026 API — addin plumbing (IExternalCommand, IExternalApplication, ribbon), transactions, geometry, family documents, curtain walls, and the in-process bridge (named-pipe listener, Roslyn compilation, ExternalEvent, WPF approval window). Use it for any task in RevitBridge.Addin or RevitBridge.Utils, or that touches RevitAPI.dll / RevitAPIUI.dll. Verifies by compiling in Debug and Release plus the xUnit suite; it cannot run Revit. ([Ashromer/prompt-to-revit](https://github.com/Ashromer/prompt-to-revit/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/revit-developer.md) · ★1)
- **revit-graph-agent**: GraphDB 및 Ontology 관리 전문 에이전트. BIM 데이터의 그래프 구조화, Neo4j 연동, 온톨로지 스키마 관리를 담당합니다. ([tygwan/AgenticREVIT](https://github.com/tygwan/AgenticREVIT/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/revit-graph-agent.md) · ★1)
- **revival-scout**: Read-only legwork for game-revival projects — sweep decompiled code / unpacked bundles for every network call, save read/write or endpoint caller; read long device logs and report the first real error; skim OCR text. Never edits files. Use for broad searches whose conclusion is all the lead needs. ([Cynthia-wlan9509/offline-game-revival](https://github.com/Cynthia-wlan9509/offline-game-revival/blob/HEAD/agents/revival-scout.md) · ★109)
- **revival-verifier**: Independently checks a claim that a game-revival change works — reruns tests, reviews the diff for out-of-scope edits and silent fallbacks, and lists what is verified vs. merely implemented. Use before telling the user something is finished. ([Cynthia-wlan9509/offline-game-revival](https://github.com/Cynthia-wlan9509/offline-game-revival/blob/HEAD/agents/revival-verifier.md) · ★109)
- **revival-worker**: Executes a clearly specified piece of a game-revival project — implement a listed group of fake-server endpoints, write timewarp/regression tests, generate data tables, refactor per an agreed plan. Not for design decisions or open-ended debugging. ([Cynthia-wlan9509/offline-game-revival](https://github.com/Cynthia-wlan9509/offline-game-revival/blob/HEAD/agents/revival-worker.md) · ★109)
- **reviwer**: Performs deep, structured code reviews of POS system features. Use when the user asks for "review of X", "review feature X", "code review of X", "audit module X", "analyze code quality of X", or any technical review request for a functionality or component of the project. ([Elianchox/pos](https://github.com/Elianchox/pos/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/reviwer.md))
- **revize-geometrie**: Nezávislá revize geometrického a nestingového kódu. Use PROACTIVELY po každém dokončeném kroku, který mění src/core/geometry nebo src/core/nesting, a v kroku 7.1. Jen čte a spouští testy, nic needituje. ([JaroslavPavela/Materialovy_optimalizator](https://github.com/JaroslavPavela/Materialovy_optimalizator/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/revize-geometrie.md))
- **revizor**: Porovná pracovní projekt s jeho požadavky a doloží nálezy bez změn. ([michaelprinc/Workshop-Agent-LLM-Task-Assignment](https://github.com/michaelprinc/Workshop-Agent-LLM-Task-Assignment/blob/HEAD/balicek-technicke/.github/agents/revizor.agent.md))
- **revizor-baza-date**: Revizuiește migrările SQL și sincronizarea lor cu tipurile generate — RLS, SECURITY DEFINER, GRANT-uri, numerotare, drift între repo și baza aplicată. Se invocă din skill-ul revizuire-erp. ([miroslav43/ERP](https://github.com/miroslav43/ERP/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/revizor-baza-date.md))
- **revizor-contracte**: Caută drift de contract între straturi — un tip, enum, coloană, schemă Zod sau semnătură schimbată într-un loc, cu consumatori rămași neactualizați în alt strat. Nu se sare niciodată. Se invocă din skill-ul revizuire-erp. ([miroslav43/ERP](https://github.com/miroslav43/ERP/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/revizor-contracte.md))
- **revizor-domeniu**: Revizuiește logica pură de calcul din src/domain — salarizare, concedii, pontaj, diurnă, calendar, REVISAL — plus acoperirea cu teste. Se invocă din skill-ul revizuire-erp. ([miroslav43/ERP](https://github.com/miroslav43/ERP/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/revizor-domeniu.md))
- **revizor-nextjs**: Revizuiește granița server/client, caching-ul, revalidarea și formularele în Next.js 16 App Router cu React 19. Se invocă din skill-ul revizuire-erp. ([miroslav43/ERP](https://github.com/miroslav43/ERP/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/revizor-nextjs.md))
- **revizor-securitate**: Revizuiește schimbările din perspectiva izolării între firme-client (multi-tenant), a ocolirii RLS prin service_role, a autorizării Server Actions și a datelor personale criptate. Se invocă din skill-ul revizuire-erp. ([miroslav43/ERP](https://github.com/miroslav43/ERP/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/revizor-securitate.md))
- **revizor-stavu**: Porovná, co je v STAV.md napsané, s tím, co se v projektu doopravdy stalo. Použij pro jeden projekt, když potřebuješ vědět, jestli se dokumentace rozešla se skutečností. Vrací seznam rozporů, nic nepřepisuje. ([vavski/dilna](https://github.com/vavski/dilna/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/revizor-stavu.md))

- **revue**: Relit uniquement les changements récents (git diff / git log) de AI Shorts Generator, jamais l'intégralité du dépôt. Repère régressions, erreurs logiques, problèmes de sécurité/maintenabilité. Ne modifie rien. - **revue-api**: Audite un diff de l'API (apps/api) contre docs/conventions/architecture-api.md - couches HTTP / métier / accès aux données, repository mal nommé ou mal découpé, règle métier dans un repository, Prisma ou type généré hors du repository, type de base qui franchit la frontière HTTP, données personnelles dans les journaux. À utiliser pendant une revue de PR touchant à apps/api, ou quand on demande un audit de l'architecture de l'API. ([betagouv/etape](https://github.com/betagouv/etape/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/revue-api.md) · ★1)

- **revue-fitcarnet**: Relit du code fitcarnet et rapporte les défauts, en lecture seule. À invoquer avant de pousser, sur une modification en cours, ou pour éprouver un pan de code. Ne modifie JAMAIS le code — il rapporte, l'auteur décide.
